REBench consolidates a superset of existing datasets, comprising hundreds of millions of lines of source code and a diverse collection of binaries spanning multiple architectures and optimization levels. REBench adopts a knowledge-base-driven methodology that stores byte-level stack information to generate ground truth, ensuring that task difficulty is preserved while maintaining universal applicability. This design enables fair evaluation across tasks while avoiding simplifications that could bias results. As a use case, we apply REBench to measure the reverse engineering performance of LLMs and the result demonstrates difficulties in complex tasks.

</details>


### [144] [Toward Autonomous SOC Operations: End-to-End LLM Framework for Threat Detection, Query Generation, and Resolution in Security Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27321)
*Md Hasan Saju,Akramul Azim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的威胁管理框架，集成集成检测、语法约束查询生成（SQM架构）和检索增强解决支持，将SOC事件分诊时间从数小时缩短至10分钟以内。检测模块通过集成三个最优LLM达到82.8%准确率和0.120假阳性率；SQM架构在IBM QRadar和Google SecOps上生成可执行查询，BLEU 0.384、ROUGE-L 0.731；结合SQM证据将解决代码预测准确率从78.3%提升至90.0%，推荐质量评分8.70。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临威胁量激增、异构SIEM平台、手动分诊流程耗时长等挑战，现有方法缺乏端到端自动化能力，且LLM在安全领域直接应用存在语法约束和可靠性不足的问题。

Method: 提出三模块框架：(1) 检测模块集成传统ML分类器和三个最优LLM构建集成模型；(2) SQM架构利用平台特定语法约束、元数据检索和文档引导提示生成可执行查询；(3) 检索增强生成模块利用SQM输出的证据提升事件解决代码预测和推荐生成质量。

Result: 检测模块准确率82.8%、假阳性率0.120；SQM BLEU 0.384、ROUGE-L 0.731，是基线LLM的两倍以上；解决代码预测准确率从78.3%提升至90.0%；推荐质量评分8.70；生产环境事件分诊时间从数小时降至10分钟以内。

Conclusion: 证明了领域约束的LLM架构结合检索增强能够在严格可靠性和效率要求下满足大规模运营安全环境的需求。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) face mounting operational challenges. These challenges come from increasing threat volumes, heterogeneous SIEM platforms, and time-consuming manual triage workflows. We present an end-to-end threat management framework that integrates ensemble-based detection, syntax-constrained query generation, and retrieval-augmented resolution support to automate critical security workflows. Our detection module evaluates both traditional machine learning classifiers and large language models (LLMs), then combines the three best-performing LLMs to create an ensemble model, achieving 82.8% accuracy while maintaining 0.120 false positive rate on SIEM logs. We introduce the SQM (Syntax Query Metadata) architecture for automated evidence collection. It uses platform-specific syntax constraints, metadata-based retrieval, and documentation-grounded prompting to generate executable queries for IBM QRadar and Google SecOps. SQM achieves a BLEU score of 0.384 and a ROUGE-L score of 0.731. These results are more than twice as good as the baseline LLM performance. For incident resolution and recommendation generation, we demonstrate that integrating SQM-derived evidence improves resolution code prediction accuracy from 78.3% to 90.0%, with an overall recommendation quality score of 8.70. In production SOC environments, our framework reduces average incident triage time from hours to under 10 minutes. This work demonstrates that domain-constrained LLM architectures with retrieval augmentation can meet the strict reliability and efficiency requirements of operational security environments at scale.

</details>


### [145] [Secret Stealing Attacks on Local LLM Fine-Tuning through Supply-Chain Model Code Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27426)
*Zi Li,Tian Zhou,Wenze Li,Jingyu Hua,Yunlong Mao,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种新型供应链攻击：通过将恶意代码伪装成标准模型架构定义，实现从被动权重投毒到主动执行劫持的范式转变，从而在本地微调过程中窃取高熵敏感秘密（如API密钥、财务记录等），攻击成功率超过98%且能绕过DP-SGD、语义审计和代码审计等防御措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有被动式预训练权重投毒攻击在自然语言任务中有效，但无法捕获稀疏高熵目标（如API密钥、个人标识符等敏感秘密），因为这些目标依赖概率性语义前缀。同时，本地离线微调常被视为隐私边界，但被攻破的模型代码足以窃取这些秘密。

Method: 利用被伪装成标准架构定义的模型代码作为供应链攻击向量，实现主动执行劫持。提出确定性全链记忆机制：通过在线张量规则匹配锁定动态计算流中的token级秘密，利用值-梯度解耦技术隐秘注入攻击梯度，克服梯度淹没问题以强制模型记忆。首次实现攻击者可验证的秘密窃取，通过黑盒查询精确区分真实泄露与幻觉。

Result: 方法在严格攻击成功率（Strict ASR）上超过98%，且不损害主任务性能。能够有效绕过DP-SGD、语义审计和代码审计等防御措施。

Conclusion: 本地离线微调并不提供真正的隐私保护，模型代码层面的供应链攻击向量需要引起重视，现有的防御措施不足以应对此类主动执行劫持攻击。

Abstract: Local fine-tuning datasets routinely contain sensitive secrets such as API keys, personal identifiers, and financial records. Although ''local offline fine-tuning'' is often viewed as a privacy boundary, we reveal that compromised model code is sufficient to steal them. Current passive pretrained-weight poisoning attacks, while effective for natural language, fundamentally fail to capture such sparse high-entropy targets due to their reliance on probabilistic semantic prefixes. To bridge this gap, we identify and exploit a practical but overlooked supply-chain vector -- model code camouflaged as standard architectural definitions -- to realize a paradigm shift from passive weight poisoning to active execution hijacking. We introduce a deterministic full-chain memorization mechanism: it locks onto token-level secrets in dynamic computation flows via online tensor-rule matching, and leverages value-gradient decoupling to stealthily inject attack gradients, overcoming gradient drowning to force model memorization. Furthermore, we achieve, for the first time, attacker-verifiable secret stealing through black-box queries that precisely distinguishes true leakage from hallucination. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves over 98\% Strict ASR without compromising the primary task, and can effectively bypass defense measures including DP-SGD, semantic auditing, and code auditing.

</details>


### [146] [Secure Cross-Silo Synthetic Genomic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27456)
*Daniil Filienko,Martine De Cock,Sikha Pentyala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合安全多方计算（MPC）和差分隐私（DP）的方法，使多个数据持有者能够在不暴露原始数据的情况下联合训练生成模型，合成高保真的隐私保护基因组数据，并在多个真实RNA-seq队列中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组数据访问受严格监管，现有数据访问流程繁琐，严重阻碍了AI方法在基因组学中的应用。同时，许多应用（如罕见病研究）中单个机构数据量不足，需要跨机构整合数据，但直接共享原始数据存在隐私泄露风险。

Method: 采用安全多方计算（MPC）保障输入隐私，确保任何参与方在训练过程中不泄露未加密的原始数据；同时引入差分隐私（DP）提供输出隐私，减轻从发布的合成数据中泄露信息的风险。两者结合实现从分布式数据中联合训练合成数据生成器。

Result: 在多个真实RNA-seq队列的联邦设置下，该方法成功生成了高可用性的合成数据集，证明了其在跨机构分布式数据场景下实现隐私保护数据合成的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的MPC+DP联合框架能够有效解决基因组数据跨机构共享中的隐私与数据规模矛盾，在保护隐私的同时保持合成数据的高效用，为基因组学AI方法开发提供了可行的数据共享方案。

Abstract: Access to genomic data is highly regulated due to its sensitive nature. While safeguards are essential, cumbersome data access processes pose a significant barrier to the development of AI methods for genomics. Synthetic data generation can mitigate this tension by enabling broader data sharing without exposing sensitive information. Synthetic genomic data are produced by training generative models on real data and subsequently sampling artificial data that preserves relevant statistics while limiting disclosures about the underlying individuals. In some settings, a single data holder may have sufficient data to train such generative models; however, in many applications data must be combined across multiple sites to achieve adequate scale. This need arises, e.g., in rare disease studies, where individual hospitals typically hold data for only a small number of patients. The solution we present in this paper enables multiple data holders to jointly train a synthetic data generator without revealing their raw data. Our approach combines secure multiparty computation (MPC) to ensure input privacy, so that no party ever discloses its data in unencrypted form, with differential privacy (DP) to provide output privacy by mitigating information leakage from the released synthetic data. We empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method by generating high-utility synthetic datasets from multiple real RNA-seq cohorts in federated settings, showing that our approach enables privacy-preserving data synthesis even when data are distributed across institutions.

</details>


### [147] [Security Attack and Defense Strategies for Autonomous Agent Frameworks: A Layered Review with OpenClaw as a Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27464)
*Luyao Xu,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对基于大语言模型（LLM）的自主智能体框架的安全问题进行了分层综述。研究将安全风险与防御策略划分为四个层次：上下文与指令层、工具与动作层、状态与持久化层、生态系统与自动化层，并以OpenClaw为案例进行分析。文章还指出了跨层威胁传播机制，以及当前研究在层间失衡、长期评估缺失和生态信任模型薄弱等方面的挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM自主智能体框架发展为集成工具、持续运行的复杂系统，其安全风险已超越传统提示级漏洞。由于该范式尚处早期发展阶段，亟需系统性地理解其安全影响。现有研究分散在智能体安全的各个孤立方面，缺乏分层综述，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文采用分层综述方法，将自主智能体框架的安全风险和防御策略组织为四个安全相关层次：上下文与指令层、工具与动作层、状态与持久化层、生态系统与自动化层。以OpenClaw为案例进行分析，并进一步识别跨层威胁传播机制。

Result: 总结了各层次的功能角色、代表性安全风险和相应防御策略；识别出威胁可能从操控输入跨层传播到不安全动作、持久化状态污染及生态系统层面的影响；指出了研究层间失衡、缺乏长期评估和生态系统信任模型薄弱等关键挑战。

Conclusion: 自主智能体框架的安全需要更系统和集成的防御方法，未来方向包括解决层间研究不平衡、建立长期评估机制以及强化生态系统信任模型。

Abstract: Autonomous agent frameworks built upon large language models (LLMs) are evolving into complex, tool-integrated, and continuously operating systems, introducing security risks beyond traditional prompt-level vulnerabilities. As this paradigm is still at an early stage of development, a timely and systematic understanding of its security implications is increasingly important. Although a growing body of work has examined different attack surfaces and defense problems in agent systems, existing studies remain scattered across individual aspects of agent security, and there is still a lack of a layered review on this topic. To address this gap, this survey presents a layered review of security risks and defense strategies in autonomous agent frameworks, with OpenClaw as a case study. We organize the analysis into four security-relevant layers: the context and instruction layer, the tool and action layer, the state and persistence layer, and the ecosystem and automation layer. For each layer, we summarize its functional role, representative security risks, and corresponding defense strategies. Based on this layered analysis, we further identify that threats in autonomous agent frameworks may propagate across layers, from manipulated inputs to unsafe actions, persistent state contamination, and broader ecosystem-level impact. Finally, we highlight potential key challenges, including research imbalance across layers, the lack of long-horizon evaluation, and weak ecosystem trust models, and outline future directions toward more systematic and integrated defenses.

</details>


### [148] [SecGoal: A Benchmark for Security Goal Extraction and Formalization from Protocol Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27601)
*Dawei Huang,Hui Li,Haonan Feng,Jingjing Guan,Yueshuang Jiao,Bo Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个专家标注的安全目标基准SecGoal（覆盖15个协议文档），以及AI辅助框架AIFG，用于从自然语言协议文档中自动提取和形式化安全目标。实验表明，指令微调后的小模型（7B/9B参数）F1分数超80%，显著优于大型通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证虽能提供严格的安全保证，但从自然语言协议文档中自动提取并形式化安全目标仍是一个主要瓶颈，且缺乏专家标注资源和整合非结构化文本与符号逻辑的框架。

Method: 构建SecGoal基准（15个协议文档），提出AIFG框架，将任务分解为上下文感知的目标提取和检索增强的形式化两个子任务。

Result: 前沿模型（如Gemini 2.5-Pro）召回率高但精确率低于15%；在SecGoal上指令微调后，7B/9B参数量的小模型F1分数超过80%。

Conclusion: 本研究为自动化形式化协议分析建立了基础数据集和可复现的基线。

Abstract: Formal verification provides rigorous guarantees for cryptographic security, yet automating the extraction and formalization of security goals from natural language protocol documents remains a major bottleneck, compounded by the scarcity of expert-annotated resources and integrated frameworks bridging unstructured text and symbolic logic. We introduce SecGoal, the first expert-annotated benchmark covering 15 widely deployed protocol documents, including 5G-AKA and TLS 1.3, and AIFG, an AI-assisted framework that decomposes the task into context-aware goal extraction and retrieval-augmented formalization. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation to assess whether contemporary LLMs are ready to automate this pipeline. Our results reveal a pronounced precision-recall imbalance: frontier models, such as Gemini 2.5-Pro, achieve high recall but precision below 15%, frequently misclassifying operational text as security goals. In contrast, instruction tuning on SecGoal enables compact models with 7B/9B parameters to achieve F1-scores above 80%, substantially outperforming larger general-purpose models. Our work establishes a foundational dataset and reproducible baseline for automated formal protocol analysis.

</details>


### [149] [VOW: Verifiable and Oblivious Watermark Detection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27666)
*Xiaokun Luan,Yihao Zhang,Pengcheng Su,Feiran Lei,Meng Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VOW是一种新型LLM水印协议，通过将检测问题转化为安全两方计算，结合可验证不经意伪随机函数(VOPRF)，实现了隐私保护和可加密验证的水印检测，适用于短文本且能抵御释义攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印方法依赖中心化信任模型，用户需向服务商暴露敏感文本，且无法验证检测结果的完整性；已有的非对称方案要么对短文本不实用，要么缺乏水印嵌入与检测之间的正式安全保证。

Method: 将水印检测形式化为安全两方计算问题，使用可验证不经意伪随机函数(VOPRF)实例化水印核心逻辑，使用户与提供商在不泄露用户文本的情况下完成检测，同时提供商的结果可被验证。

Result: 实验表明VOW对短文本具有实用性，并提供了针对现代释义攻击的水印鲁棒性的重要重新评估。

Conclusion: VOW在隐私保护、可验证性和效率之间取得了良好平衡，是首个同时实现隐私保护与加密可验证的高效水印检测协议。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) watermarking is crucial for establishing the provenance of machine-generated text, but most existing methods rely on a centralized trust model. This model forces users to reveal potentially sensitive text to a provider for detection and offers no way to verify the integrity of the result. While asymmetric schemes have been proposed to address these issues, they are either impractical for short texts or lack formal guarantees linking watermark insertion and detection. We propose VOW, a new protocol that achieves both privacy-preserving and cryptographically verifiable watermark detection with high efficiency. Our approach formulates detection as a secure two-party computation problem, instantiating the watermark's core logic with a Verifiable Oblivious Pseudorandom Function (VOPRF). This allows the user and provider to perform detection without the user's text being revealed, while the provider's result is verifiable. Our comprehensive evaluation shows that VOW is practical for short texts and provides a crucial reassessment of watermark robustness against modern paraphrasing attacks.

</details>


### [150] [How Code Representation Shapes False-Positive Dynamics in Cross-Language LLM Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27714)
*Maofei Chen,Laifu Wang,Yue Qin,Yuan Wang,Bo Wu,Dongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了代码表示格式（原始文本 vs 剪枝AST）对跨语言LLM漏洞检测中误报率（FPR）的影响，发现文本微调通过表面线索记忆机制导致FPR单调上升，而AST表示可缓解此问题；作者提出了一种无需重训练的文本-AST一致性门控过滤策略来降低误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对于代码表示格式如何影响跨语言LLM漏洞检测中的误报行为缺乏系统理解，尤其是训练时和推理时表示格式的联合效应尚不明确。

Method: 系统变化训练强度和代码表示格式（原始源代码 vs 剪枝AST），在训练和推理时分别组合，基于两个8B参数LLM（Qwen3-8B和Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct）在C/C++ NIST Juliet数据集上微调，在Java OWASP和Python Benchmark上评估。设计了跨表示探测实验（文本训练权重直接用于AST编码输入）。

Result: 文本微调使FPR单调上升（Qwen3-8B: 0.763→0.866→1.000），F1保持稳定（0.637-0.688）；跨表示探测使FPR从0.866降至0.583，37.2%误报转为真负；直接AST微调FPR≥0.970；两个模型族结果一致；Python结果与Java相差仅2.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 跨语言误报是训练时和推理时表示格式的联合效应，表面线索记忆是主要机制；提出预部署一致性门控（同时通过文本和AST路径）作为无需重训练的误报过滤方法，代价是召回率降低。

Abstract: How code representation format shapes false positive behaviour in cross-language LLM vulnerability detection remains poorly understood. We systematically vary training intensity and code representation format, comparing raw source text with pruned Abstract Syntax Trees at both training time and inference time, across two 8B-parameter LLMs (Qwen3-8B and Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct) fine-tuned on C/C++ data from the NIST Juliet Test Suite (v1.3) and evaluated on Java (OWASP Benchmark v1.2) and Python (BenchmarkPython v0.1).
  Cross-language FPR reflects the joint effect of training-time and inference-time representation, not either alone. Text fine-tuning drives FPR upward monotonically (Qwen3-8B: 0.763 zero-shot, 0.866 pilot, 1.000 full-scale) while F1 remains stable (0.637-0.688), masking the collapse. We argue surface-cue memorisation is the primary mechanism: text fine-tuning encodes C/C++-specific API names and syntactic idioms as vulnerability triggers that fire indiscriminately on target-language code. A cross-representation probe, applying text-trained weights to AST-encoded input without retraining, isolates this: Qwen3-8B FPR drops from 0.866 to 0.583, and 37.2% of false positives revert to true negatives under AST input alone. Direct AST fine-tuning does not preserve the benefit (FPR at least 0.970), as flat linearisation introduces structural surface cues of its own. The pattern replicates across both model families. On BenchmarkPython the AST probe yields FPR=0.554, within 2.9 percentage points of the Java result, despite maximal surface-syntax differences, substantially weakening a domain-shift explanation. These findings motivate a pre-deployment consistency gate, running alerts through both text and AST paths, as a retraining-free filter for false-positive-sensitive settings, at the cost of reduced recall.

</details>


### [151] [Variational and Majorization Principles in Lattice Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27801)
*Javier Blanco-Romero,Florina Almenares Mendoza*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文利用majorization理论描述格基约化中Lovász交换的平滑机制，证明每个非退化交换对应log-norm轮廓上的T-变换，进而推出最坏情况GSA包络的变分解释和方差耗散的精确伸缩恒等式。基于此提出Thermal-Adaptive和Geodesic Deep-LLL两种选择器，在基准测试中分别降低操作次数和等效交换次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解格基约化中Lovász交换如何平滑Gram-Schmidt轮廓，并利用majorization理论给出严格的数学描述。

Method: 使用majorization理论将Lovász交换建模为log-norm轮廓上的T-变换，利用Schur-凸函数度量轮廓扩散程度，推导GSA包络的变分解释和方差耗散恒等式，并基于此设计热力学族Schur-凸评分规则及两种具体选择器。

Result: 最坏情况GSA包络具有变分解释，是唯一与Lovász间隙几何兼容的最小方差轮廓；实现了方差耗散的精确伸缩恒等式；Thermal-Adaptive在平坦轮廓上减少操作次数，在q-ary输入上恢复SS-GG性能；Geodesic Deep-LLL在结构化格上减少等效交换次数但增加墙钟时间。

Conclusion: Majorization理论为格基约化中的局部交换机制提供了统一分析框架，并启发设计了更高效的交换选择策略。

Abstract: Lattice reduction smooths the Gram-Schmidt profile, and we use majorization to describe the local swap mechanism behind that smoothing. In this language, each non-degenerate Lovász swap acts as a T-transform on the log-norm profile. As a consequence, every strictly Schur-convex measure of profile spread decreases at such a swap. Two structural consequences follow. First, the worst-case GSA envelope admits a variational interpretation. It is the unique minimum-variance profile compatible with the Lovász gap geometry, so its slope is determined by the LLL parameter alone. Second, the realized swap trajectory satisfies an exact telescoping identity for variance dissipation. The same viewpoint also helps organize deep-insertion heuristics. It suggests a thermal family of Schur-convex scoring rules, motivates adaptive selection within that family, and leads to two concrete selectors: Thermal-Adaptive, which reduces operation counts relative to SS-GG on flat profiles in our benchmarks while recovering SS-GG on $q$-ary inputs, and Geodesic Deep-LLL, which reduces equivalent-swap counts on structured lattices in our benchmarks at higher wall-clock cost.

</details>


### [152] [MASCing: Configurable Mixture-of-Experts Behavior via Activation Steering Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27818)
*Jona te Lintelo,Lichao Wu,Marina Krček,Sengim Karayalçin,Stjepan Picek*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MASCing 是首个无需重训练即可灵活重配 MoE 大模型安全行为的框架，利用 LSTM 替代模型捕捉路由依赖，优化转向矩阵并在推理时通过掩码覆盖专家选择，在多轮越狱防御中平均防御成功率从 52.5% 提升至 83.9%，成人内容生成成功率从 52.6% 提升至 82.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 架构的稀疏激活特性使模型行为与路由决策耦合，在安全相关场景中产生难以控制的机制；同时，针对不同安全目标通过全微调或重训练来适配模型行为成本高昂。

Method: 使用 LSTM 替代模型捕获跨层路由依赖并将路由 logits 映射到下游行为；优化转向矩阵以识别行为相关专家电路；推理时对路由门控施加转向掩码以覆盖专家选择。

Result: 多轮越狱防御：平均防御成功率从 52.5% 提升至 83.9%（最高 89.2%）；成人内容生成：平均生成成功率从 52.6% 提升至 82.0%（最高 93.0%）。在七个开源 MoE 模型上取得一致提升，且开销可忽略。

Conclusion: MASCing 是一种实用、轻量且灵活的框架，适用于 MoE 模型中按场景的安全行为重配置，无需重新训练。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures in Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly reduced inference costs through sparse activation. However, this sparse activation paradigm also introduces new safety challenges. Since only a subset of experts is engaged for each input, model behavior becomes coupled to routing decisions, yielding a difficult-to-control mechanism that can vary across safety-relevant scenarios. At the same time, adapting model behavior through full fine-tuning or retraining is costly, especially when developers need to rapidly configure the same model for different safety objectives. We present MASCing (MoE Activation Steering Configuration), the first framework that enables flexible reconfiguration of MoE behavior across diverse safety scenarios without retraining. MASCing uses an LSTM-based surrogate model to capture cross-layer routing dependencies and map routing logits to downstream behaviors. It then optimizes a steering matrix to identify behavior-relevant expert circuits and, at inference time, applies steering masks to the routing gates to override expert selection. This enables targeted enhancement or suppression of specific behaviors while preserving general language utility. To demonstrate its reconfigurability, we apply MASCing to two different safety-related objectives and observe consistent gains with negligible overhead across seven open-source MoE models. For multi-turn jailbreak defense, it improves the average defense success rate from 52.5% to 83.9%, with gains of up to 89.2%. For adult-content generation, MASCing enables models to comply with such requests that would otherwise be refused, increasing the average generation success rate from 52.6% to 82.0%, with gains of up to 93.0%. These results establish MASCing as a practical, lightweight, and flexible framework for scenario-specific safety reconfiguration in MoE models.

</details>


### [153] [WOOTdroid: Whole-system Online On-device Tracing for Android](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27830)
*Simon Althaus,Nikolaos Alexopoulos,Max Mühlhäuser,Christian Reuter,Ephraim Zimmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WOOTdroid 提出了一种无需修改操作系统或应用插桩的 Android 设备端追踪方案，通过 eBPF 实现的 WDSys 解决 syscall 丢事件问题，通过 WDBind 在内核层捕获并解码 Binder parcels 以弥合语义鸿沟，在 Pixel 9/Android 16 上验证了十个安全相关 Binder 事务的重建能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android 系统审计面临两大问题：一是现有 syscall 追踪器在高负载下丢事件；二是安全相关的应用行为通过 Binder IPC 实现，对 syscall 层不可见，存在语义鸿沟。现有方案要么修改 Android 平台难以适配系统更新，要么在用户空间插桩应用可被高级攻击者绕过。

Method: WOOTdroid 包含两个组件：WDSys 基于 eBPF 移植了 eAudit 风格的 syscall 审计，运行在未修改的 Android 内核上；WDBind 在内核中捕获 Binder parcels，通过 Java 反射提取的框架签名表在进程外解码这些数据包。

Result: WDSys 在 Geekbench 测试中最多带来 3.6% 的性能开销，比 ftrace 多追踪 33% 的 syscall。在 Pixel 9/Android 16 上通过端到端案例研究成功重建了十个安全相关的 Binder 事务。

Conclusion: WOOTdroid 在不修改操作系统或进行应用插桩的前提下，同时解决了 Android 系统审计中的丢事件和语义鸿沟问题，为设备端追踪提供了一种实用的原型方案。

Abstract: System auditing on Android faces two problems. First, existing syscall tracers lose events under load, silently overwriting entries faster than a user space reader can drain them. Second, security-relevant application behavior is mediated through Binder, Android's kernel IPC mechanism, and is therefore hidden from the syscall layer. The Binder parcels that the kernel does see carry no method names or typed arguments, a disconnect between low-level events and high-level behavior known as the semantic gap. Existing approaches address the semantic gap either by modifying the Android platform, making them difficult to adjust to OS updates, or by instrumenting the traced application in user space, which sophisticated adversaries can evade by bypassing the instrumented framework APIs.
  We present WOOTdroid, a design and prototype for on-device tracing on stock Android that addresses both problems without OS modification or application instrumentation. WDSys, an eBPF port of eAudit-style syscall auditing, runs on current Android with at most 3.6% Geekbench overhead and traces 33% more syscalls than ftrace. WDBind captures Binder parcels in the kernel and decodes them out-of-process against a framework signature table extracted via Java reflection. We demonstrate WOOTdroid on Pixel 9 devices running Android 16 with an end-to-end case study reconstructing ten security-relevant Binder transactions.

</details>


### [154] [TwinGate: Stateful Defense against Decompositional Jailbreaks in Untraceable Traffic via Asymmetric Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27861)
*Bowen Sun,Chaozhuo Li,Yaodong Yang,Yiwei Wang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TwinGate提出了一种状态化的双编码器防御框架，通过非对称对比学习将语义不同但意图匹配的恶意片段聚类到共享潜在空间，在低延迟开销下有效抵御分解式越狱攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御策略在完全匿名化、任意交织的请求流威胁模型下，无法追踪全局历史上下文，且实时监控的生成模型带来计算开销过高的问题。

Method: 提出TwinGate双编码器框架：采用非对称对比学习将语义不同但意图匹配的恶意片段聚类到共享潜在空间；并行使用冻结编码器抑制良性主题重叠导致的误报；每个请求仅需单次轻量前向传播，与目标模型预填充阶段并行执行。

Result: 在超过362万条指令、8600种恶意意图的大规模数据集上，在严格因果协议下实现了高恶意意图召回率和极低误报率，对自适应攻击具有强鲁棒性，在吞吐量和延迟方面优于状态化和无状态基线。

Conclusion: TwinGate通过高效的状态化双编码器设计，在可接受的延迟开销下显著提升了对分解式越狱攻击的防御能力，为LLM安全部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Decompositional jailbreaks pose a critical threat to large language models (LLMs) by allowing adversaries to fragment a malicious objective into a sequence of individually benign queries that collectively reconstruct prohibited content. In real-world deployments, LLMs face a continuous, untraceable stream of fully anonymized and arbitrarily interleaved requests, infiltrated by covertly distributed adversarial queries. Under this rigorous threat model, state-of-the-art defensive strategies exhibit fundamental limitations. In the absence of trustworthy user metadata, they are incapable of tracking global historical contexts, while their deployment of generative models for real-time monitoring introduces computationally prohibitive overhead. To address this, we present TwinGate, a stateful dual-encoder defense framework. TwinGate employs Asymmetric Contrastive Learning (ACL) to cluster semantically disparate but intent-matched malicious fragments in a shared latent space, while a parallel frozen encoder suppresses false positives arising from benign topical overlap. Each request requires only a single lightweight forward pass, enabling the defense to execute in parallel with the target model's prefill phase at negligible latency overhead. To evaluate our approach and advance future research, we construct a comprehensive dataset of over 3.62 million instructions spanning 8,600 distinct malicious intents. Evaluated on this large-scale corpus under a strictly causal protocol, TwinGate achieves high malicious intent recall at a remarkably low false positive rate while remaining highly robust against adaptive attacks. Furthermore, our proposal substantially outperforms stateful and stateless baselines, delivering superior throughput and reduced latency.

</details>


### [155] [Latent Adversarial Detection: Adaptive Probing of LLM Activations for Multi-Turn Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28129)
*Prashant Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"对抗性不安"概念——多轮提示注入攻击在模型残差流中留下激活级轨迹信号，通过五个标量轨迹特征将对话级检测从76.2%提升至93.8%，并系统分析了跨模型泛化性、数据源依赖性及标签粒度要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本级防御无法检测逐轮看似良性的隐蔽多轮提示注入攻击，亟需更底层的检测信号。

Method: 提取残差流中五维标量轨迹特征（表征激活向量路径长度等），训练模型特定探测器进行二阶段（信任建立/迂回/升级）攻击检测。

Result: 合成留出数据上检测率从76.2%升至93.8%；跨四族模型（24B-70B）复现但探测器不跨架构迁移；三源联合训练达89.4%检测率/2.4%误报率；二元标签导致50-59%误报。

Conclusion: 对抗性不安是可靠的激活级检测信号，实际部署需三源训练和三阶段轮级标签。

Abstract: Multi-turn prompt injection follows a known attack path -- trust-building, pivoting, escalation but text-level defenses miss covert attacks where individual turns appear benign. We show this attack path leaves an activation-level signature in the model's residual stream: each phase shift moves the activation, producing a total path length far exceeding benign conversations. We call this adversarial restlessness. Five scalar trajectory features capturing this signal lift conversation-level detection from 76.2% to 93.8% on synthetic held-out data. The signal replicates across four model families (24B-70B); probes are model-specific and do not transfer across architectures. Generalization is source-dependent: leave-one-source-out evaluation shows each of synthetic, LMSYS-Chat-1M, and SafeDialBench captures distinct attack distributions, with detection on real-world LMSYS reaching 47-71% when its distribution is represented in training. Combined three-source training achieves 89.4% detection at 2.4% false positive rate on a held-out mixed set. We further show that three-phase turn-level labels(benign/pivoting/adversarial) unique to our synthetic dataset are essential: binary conversation-level labels produce 50-59% false positives. These results establish adversarial restlessness as a reliable activation-level signal and characterize the data requirements for practical deployment.

</details>


### [156] [FlashRT: Towards Computationally and Memory Efficient Red-Teaming for Prompt Injection and Knowledge Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28157)
*Yanting Wang,Chenlong Yin,Ying Chen,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlashRT是首个针对长上下文LLM的优化型红队攻击加速框架，相比baseline nanoGCG实现2-7倍速度提升和2-4倍GPU内存降低（如32K token上下文从264.1GB降至65.7GB），可广泛应用于TAP、AutoDAN等黑盒优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化型红队攻击方法虽比启发式方法更强，但资源消耗巨大（计算和GPU内存），尤其对于长上下文场景，严重阻碍了学术界系统评估长上下文LLM安全风险及防御策略的效果。

Method: 提出FlashRT框架，通过优化计算和内存效率来加速针对长上下文LLM的优化型提示注入和知识腐败攻击，可泛化应用于多种黑盒优化方法。

Result: 与SOTA基线nanoGCG相比，FlashRT实现2-7倍加速（如从1小时降至10分钟内）和2-4倍GPU内存降低（如32K token上下文从264.1GB降至65.7GB）。

Conclusion: FlashRT可作为高效的红队工具，支持对长上下文LLM安全性的系统评估，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-context large language models (LLMs)-for example, Gemini-3.1-Pro and Qwen-3.5-are widely used to empower many real-world applications, such as retrieval-augmented generation, autonomous agents, and AI assistants. However, security remains a major concern for their widespread deployment, with threats such as prompt injection and knowledge corruption. To quantify the security risks faced by LLMs under these threats, the research community has developed heuristic-based and optimization-based red-teaming methods. Optimization-based methods generally produce stronger attacks than heuristic attacks and thus provide a more rigorous assessment of LLM security risks. However, they are often resource-intensive, requiring significant computation and GPU memory, especially for long context scenarios. The resource-intensive nature poses a major obstacle for the community (especially academic researchers) to systematically evaluate the security risks of long-context LLMs and assess the effectiveness of defense strategies at scale. In this work, we propose FlashRT, the first framework to improve the efficiency (in terms of both computation and memory) for optimization-based prompt injection and knowledge corruption attacks under long-context LLMs. Through extensive evaluations, we find that FlashRT consistently delivers a 2x-7x speedup (e.g., reducing runtime from one hour to less than ten minutes) and a 2x-4x reduction in GPU memory consumption (e.g., reducing from 264.1 GB to 65.7 GB GPU memory for a 32K token context) compared to state-of-the-art baseline nanoGCG. FlashRT can be broadly applied to black-box optimization methods, such as TAP and AutoDAN. We hope FlashRT can serve as a red-teaming tool to enable systematic evaluation of long-context LLM security. The code is available at: https://github.com/Wang-Yanting/FlashRT

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [SCOPE-FE: Structured Control of Operator and Pairwise Exploration for Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27025)
*Minhee Park,Seongyeon Son,Yonghyun Lee,Eunchan Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: SCOPE-FE通过结构化搜索空间控制（算子剪枝+特征聚类）在保持预测性能的同时大幅降低自动特征工程的计算开销，尤其适用于高维数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有expand-and-reduce方法（如OpenFE）随着输入维度增长，候选特征的组合爆炸导致计算成本急剧上升，需要更高效的搜索空间控制策略。

Method: 提出SCOPE-FE框架，包含三个核心模块：1) OperatorProbing：通过估计算子对数据集的效用，提前淘汰低贡献算子；2) FeatureClustering：利用谱嵌入和模糊C均值聚类将结构相关特征分组，限制候选生成于簇内组合；3) ReliabilityScoring：引入子样本方差稳定剪枝决策。

Result: 在10个基准数据集上，SCOPE-FE显著减少了特征工程时间，同时保持了与现有基线相当的预测性能，在高维数据集上效率提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 结构化搜索空间控制是实现可扩展自动特征工程的有效策略。

Abstract: Automatic feature engineering is an effective approach for improving predictive performance in tabular learning. However, expand-and-reduce methods, such as OpenFE, become increasingly computationally expensive as the input dimensionality grows. This limitation arises primarily from the combinatorial explosion of candidate features generated through operator-feature combinations. To address this issue, we propose SCOPE-FE, a structured search space control framework that improves efficiency by reducing the candidate space prior to feature generation. SCOPE-FE jointly regulates two major sources of combinatorial growth: the operator space and feature-pair space. First, OperatorProbing estimates the dataset-specific utility of candidate operators and eliminates low-contribution operators in advance. Second, FeatureClustering employs spectral embedding and fuzzy c-means clustering to group structurally related features, thereby restricting candidate generation to relevant within-cluster combinations. In addition, we introduce ReliabilityScoring, which incorporates variance across subsamples to stabilize pruning decisions. Experiments on ten benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCOPE-FE substantially reduces feature engineering time while maintaining competitive predictive performance relative to existing baselines. The efficiency gains are particularly pronounced for high-dimensional datasets. These results indicate that structured control of the search space is an effective strategy for scalable automatic feature engineering. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [158] [A Novel Computational Framework for Causal Inference: Tree-Based Discretization with ILP-Based Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27307)
*Tianyu Yang,Md. Noor-E-Alam*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合基于树的离散化技术与整数线性规划匹配算法的因果推断方法，通过分层确保近似线性关系并优化全局平衡，在计算效率和ATT估计偏差上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推断方法在可解释性与计算效率之间存在权衡，且匹配算法往往难以同时保证局部线性关系和全局平衡，导致估计偏差较大。

Method: 采用面向因果推断的树基离散化技术在分层内确保控制数据集近似线性关系，并结合整数线性规划匹配算法优化全局平衡，从而实现高效匹配。

Result: 相比现有最先进算法，所提方法在计算效率上更优，且ATT（平均处理效应）估计偏差更小。

Conclusion: 通过实证评估验证了该方法在因果推断场景中相较于现有技术的实际优势。

Abstract: Causal inference is essential for data-driven decision-making, as it aims to uncover causal relationships from observational data. However, identifying causality remains challenging due to the potential for confounding and the distinction between correlation and causation. While recent advances in causal machine learning and matching algorithms have improved estimation accuracy, these methods often face trade-offs between interpretability and computational efficiency. This paper proposes a novel approach that combines a tree-based discretization technique, tailored for causal inference, with an integer linear programming-based matching algorithm. The discretization ensures approximately linear relationships for control datasets within strata, enabling effective matching, while the optimization framework optimizes for global balance. The resulting algorithm yields computational efficiency and less biased ATT estimates compared to state-of-the-art algorithms. Empirical evaluations demonstrate the proposed method's practical advantages over existing techniques in causal inference scenarios.

</details>


### [159] [Bayesian X-Learner: Calibrated Posterior Inference for Heterogeneous Treatment Effects under Heavy-Tailed Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27394)
*Eichi Uehara*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯X-Learner，通过Welsch重下降伪似然实现MCMC后验推断，在保持异质性处理效应估计和校准不确定性的同时，对重尾污染数据具有鲁棒性。在IHDP基准上取得有竞争力结果，在高达20-25%污染尾部的生成数据上RMSE≈0.13且覆盖率达83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中的CATE估计需要同时满足三个性质：异质性效应、校准的不确定性以及对重尾污染数据的鲁棒性。现有元学习器满足(i)，因果森林和BART满足(i)-(ii)但依赖高斯尾部假设，没有广泛使用的工具能同时满足所有三个性质。

Method: 在交叉拟合的双稳健伪结果基础上构建X-Learner，使用Welsch重下降伪似然进行完整MCMC后验推断。针对污染数据，提出contamination_severity扩展，根据Huber极小最大δ关系选择Huber-δ nuisance损失函数，并通过贝叶斯自助抽样nuisance draws实现模块化贝叶斯池化。

Result: IHDP基准上均值√ε_PEHE=0.56（5次重复最低均值，但与基线差异不显著）。在20-25%尾部密度的污染DGP上，RMSE≈0.13，30个种子的单次交叉拟合覆盖率为83%（Wilson方法66%-93%），模块化贝叶斯池化可恢复名义95%覆盖率。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯X-Learner是首个能同时满足异质性效应估计、校准不确定性和重尾鲁棒性的广泛可用工具；在污染数据上表现显著优于现有方法，且通过模块化贝叶斯可恢复正确覆盖。

Abstract: Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE) estimation in practice demands three properties simultaneously: heterogeneous effects $τ(x)$, calibrated uncertainty over them, and robustness to the heavy tails that contaminate real outcome data. Meta-learners (Künzel et al., 2019) give (i); causal forests and BART give (i)-(ii) with Gaussian-tail assumptions; no widely used tool gives all three. We present Bayesian X-Learner, an X-Learner built on cross-fitted doubly robust pseudo-outcomes (Kennedy, 2020) with a full MCMC posterior over $τ(x)$ via a Welsch redescending pseudo-likelihood. On Hill's IHDP benchmark the default configuration attains mean $\sqrt{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{PEHE}}} = 0.56$ on 5 replications (lowest mean; differences from S-/T-/X-learners, full-config Causal BART, and a causal forest baseline are not significant at $α=0.05$, and rank ordering is unstable at 10 replications -- IHDP comparisons are competitive rather than dominant). On contaminated "whale" DGPs with up to 20-25% tail density, a one-flag extension (contamination_severity) that selects a Huber-$δ$ nuisance loss per Huber's minimax-$δ$ relation recovers RMSE $\approx 0.13$ with tight credible intervals (single-cross-fit 30-seed coverage 83% [Wilson 66%, 93%] at 20% density; modular-Bayes pooling with Bayesian-bootstrap nuisance draws restores nominal 95% coverage).

</details>


### [160] [Prediction-powered Inference by Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27892)
*Yanwu Gu,Linglong Kong,Dong Xia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合专家模型（MOE）的半监督推断框架，通过集成多个AI预测工具并利用预测驱动推断（PPI）的方差缩减原理，在有限标注数据场景下实现更高效的统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI预测工具种类繁多但各有优劣，而半监督推断中标注数据稀缺、未标注数据丰富，现有PPI方法仅依赖单一预测器，未能充分利用多个预测器的集体预测能力。

Method: 将多个预测器视为混合专家模型，基于PPI的方差缩减原理，通过优化选择专家权重组合使得推断方差最小化，适用于均值估计、线性回归、分位数估计及一般M估计。

Result: 建立了非渐近理论，给出了置信区间覆盖误差的上界；数值实验验证了方法的有效性并与理论结果一致。

Conclusion: MOE-powered推断框架相比标准PPI能自适应不同预测器的性能，享受最佳专家保证，具有更好的统计效率和灵活性。

Abstract: The rapidly expanding artificial intelligence (AI) industry has produced diverse yet powerful prediction tools, each with its own network architecture, training strategy, data-processing pipeline, and domain-specific strengths. These tools create new opportunities for semi-supervised inference, in which labeled data are limited and expensive to obtain, whereas unlabeled data are abundant and widely available. Given a collection of predictors, we treat them as a mixture of experts (MOE) and introduce an MOE-powered semi-supervised inference framework built upon prediction-powered inference (PPI). Motivated by the variance reduction principle underlying PPI, the proposed framework seeks the mixture of experts that achieves the smallest possible variance. Compared with standard PPI, the MOE-powered inference framework adapts to the unknown performance of individual predictors, benefits from their collective predictive power, and enjoys a best-expert guarantee. The framework is flexible and applies to mean estimation, linear regression, quantile estimation, and general M-estimation. We develop non-asymptotic theory for the MOE-powered inference framework and establish upper bounds on the coverage error of the resulting confidence intervals. Numerical experiments demonstrate the practical effectiveness of MOE-powered inference and corroborate our theoretical findings.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Adaptive Robust Confidence Intervals in Efron's Gaussian Two-Groups Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26992)
*Qiaosen Wang,Shuwen Chai,Chao Gao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究Efron高斯二群模型下对零位置参数θ的鲁棒置信区间。当污染比例ε未知时，置信区间的最优极小极大长度为σ(n^{-1/4}+ε^{1/2}/max{1, log(en ε^2)}^{1/2})，比ε已知时差（多项式级）。方差未知时进一步退化为Ω(σ n^{-1/8})。算法上提出基于Carathéodory半正定约束的傅里叶认证过程，达到极小极大下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实验科学中，测量协议受良好控制，污染更可能在上游引入（噪声不知情对抗）。现有鲁棒推断多基于Huber模型（任意ε比例污染），但未考虑这种噪声不知情特性。本文旨在刻画在此类对抗下，置信区间长度的最优自适应速率。

Method: 理论分析：在Efron高斯二群模型下（ε比例样本均值偏移，所有样本共享方差σ²的加性高斯测量噪声），推导适应未知ε的置信区间极小极大最优长度。算法：提出基于Carathéodory半正定约束的傅里叶认证过程，通过扫描候选点并检验其残差特征函数与高斯位置混合的相容性来构建置信区间。

Result: 1) ε未知时，最优置信区间长度为σ(n^{-1/4}+ε^{1/2}/max{1, log(en ε^2)}^{1/2})，比ε已知时多项变差。2) σ²也未知时，长度下界为Ω(σ n^{-1/8})，进一步退化。3) 所提算法在方差已知情形下达到极小极大下界且多项式时间可计算。

Conclusion: 与点估计不同（ε未知不恶化估计速率），置信区间的自适应推断在ε未知时会显著变差，且方差未知使问题更困难。这表明鲁棒不确定性量化比鲁棒点估计本质上更具挑战性。

Abstract: Robust uncertainty quantification is increasingly important in modern data analysis and is often formalized under Huber's model, which allows an $\varepsilon$-fraction of arbitrary corruptions. In many experimental sciences, however, the measurement protocol is well controlled, and contamination is more plausibly introduced upstream. Motivated by this noise-oblivious nature of adversaries, we study confidence intervals for the null location parameter $θ$ in Efron's Gaussian two-groups model, where an unknown fraction $\varepsilon$ of observations have arbitrarily shifted means, but all samples share the same law of additive Gaussian measurement noise with variance $σ^2$. We characterize the minimax-optimal length among confidence intervals with a prescribed coverage level uniformly over the unknown contamination proportion and all noise-oblivious adversaries. Although prior work has shown that the minimax point estimation rate of theta does not deteriorate when $\varepsilon$ becomes unknown, our results reveal that, with a given $σ^2$, the minimax-optimal length of confidence intervals that are adaptive to unknown $\varepsilon$ is of order $σ(n^{-1/4}+\varepsilon^{1/2}/\max\{1, \log(en \varepsilon^2)\}^{1/2})$, which is polynomially worse than the optimal length when $\varepsilon$ is known. When the variance $σ^2$ is also unknown, we show a further degradation: no adaptive robust confidence interval can be shorter than $Ω(σn^{-1/8})$. Algorithmically, we introduce a Fourier-based certification procedure built on Carathéodory's positive-semidefiniteness constraints. By scanning candidate points and accepting those whose residual characteristic function is certifiably consistent with a Gaussian location mixture, our algorithm attains the minimax lower bound in the known-variance setting and is computable in polynomial time.

</details>


### [162] [The Bernstein-von Mises theorem for Bayesian one-pass online learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27442)
*Jeyong Lee,Junhyeok Choi,Dongguen Kim,Minwoo Chae*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于单次遍历设置的新型贝叶斯在线学习算法（含预热启动阶段），证明了其后验分布能达到最优收敛速率，并建立了在线版本的Bernstein-von Mises定理，实现了无需小批量样本量发散的可靠不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯在线学习的理论保证通常要求小批量样本量趋于无穷，但这在单次遍历（one-pass）设置中无法满足。因此，需要设计适用于该设置的新算法并建立相应的理论框架。

Method: 提出一种包含预热启动（warm-start）阶段的新贝叶斯在线学习算法，以确保顺序更新的稳定性；基于一种全新的理论框架进行分析，区别于现有在线学习文献的方法。

Result: 证明了该算法下顺序更新的后验分布达到最优收敛速率；建立了在线版本的Bernstein-von Mises定理，确保无需发散的小批量样本量即可实现有效的不确定性量化；在广义线性模型上的数值实验表明该方法匹配批处理估计器的性能且优于现有在线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了单次遍历贝叶斯在线学习理论方面的空白，为无需大容量小批量的可靠在线推断提供了理论与算法基础。

Abstract: Bayesian online learning provides a coherent framework for sequential inference. However, its theoretical understanding remains limited, particularly in the one-pass setting. Existing theoretical guarantees typically require the mini-batch sample size to diverge, a condition that fails in the one-pass regime. In this paper, we propose a new Bayesian online learning algorithm tailored to the one-pass setting, which incorporates a warm-start phase to ensure stable sequential updates. For this algorithm, we show that the sequentially updated posterior attains the optimal convergence rate. Building on this, we establish an online analogue of the Bernstein-von Mises theorem, which guarantees valid uncertainty quantification without diverging mini-batch sample sizes. Our analysis is based on a novel theoretical framework that differs fundamentally from existing approaches in the online learning literature. Numerical experiments on generalized linear models show that the proposed method matches the performance of the batch estimator while outperforming existing online procedures.

</details>


### [163] [Technical Note on Relating Scores of Tilted Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27196)
*Curtis McDonald*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文扩展了线性倾斜参考测度的结果，证明恒定负对角倾斜也可将正态卷积下的分数表示为原始密度卷积的形式。通过Tweedie公式连接两种密度的去噪器，发现线性倾斜导致分数算子的位置偏移，二次倾斜则同时产生位置偏移和时间偏移。倾斜密度的分数可理解为原始卷积过程在不同位置和噪声水平下的分数，这对基于分数的扩散模型研究具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已证明线性倾斜下正态卷积分数可表示为原始密度卷积形式，本文旨在将该结果推广至恒定负对角倾斜情形，从而更全面地理解倾斜密度与原始密度分数之间的关系。

Method: 通过Tweedie公式将两种密度的去噪器联系起来，分别分析线性倾斜和二次倾斜对分数算子的影响：线性倾斜仅导致位置偏移，二次倾斜同时导致位置偏移和时间（噪声水平）偏移。

Result: 成功将线性倾斜结果扩展到恒定负对角倾斜，证明倾斜密度的分数可以理解为原始卷积过程在不同位置和噪声水平下的分数。

Conclusion: 该发现对基于分数的扩散模型社区有价值，可能引领利用这些倾斜分数关系构建更好的分数估计器。

Abstract: Recent results have shown that for a linear tilt to a reference measure, the scores that would be produced under convolution with a normal variable can be expressed in terms of convolutions of the original density. Here, we extend that result to include constant negative diagonal tilts as well. The relationship follows from relating the denoisers of the two densities, which define the scores via Tweedie formula. A linear tilt results in a location shift to the score operator, while a quadratic tilt results in both a location shift and a time shift. Thus the scores of the tilted density can be understood as the scores of the original convolution process at a different location and noise level. These results are of interest to those in the score based diffusion community, and may lead to better score estimators which take advantage of these tilted score relationships.

</details>


### [164] [A note on estimation of quarticity based on spot volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27665)
*Yi Guo*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对连续Itô半鞅的四次变差（quarticity）估计问题，提出了一种更直观的估计量，建立了其中心极限定理（收敛速度为$1/\sqrt{\Delta_n}$，稳定收敛意义下），并与现有估计量的渐近方差进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四次变差估计量在直观性和理解上存在不足，论文旨在提出一种更易于理解和使用的估计量。

Method: 提出一种新的、更直观的四次变差估计量，推导其在稳定收敛意义下的中心极限定理，并与经典估计量进行渐近方差比较。

Result: 新估计量满足中心极限定理，收敛速度为$1/\sqrt{\Delta_n}$；通过与现有估计量的渐近方差比较，展示了其相对效率。

Conclusion: 提出的直观估计量在理论上具有良好性质，为连续Itô半鞅的四次变差估计提供了一种有效替代方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we aim at estimating the quarticity of continuous Itô semimartingales. Instead of using some classical estimators, we introduce a more intuitive one and establish a central limit theorem (CLT) for it, with a convergence rate of $1/\sqrt{Δ_n}$ in the sense of stable convergence. Moreover, we compare the asymptotic variance of this estimator with that of other existing estimators.

</details>


### [165] [A High Dimensional Wild Bootstrap Max-Test for Detecting the Presence of Significant Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27813)
*Jonathan B. Hill*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于块自助法的最大检验统计量，用于在高维弱依赖、异质（可能非平稳）时间序列数据中检测显著预测变量。该方法无需协方差矩阵估计和自适应重采样，通过最大统计量规避了非均匀渐近性和Bonferroni校正问题，并利用乘子（wild）块自助法逼近非标准极限分布。数值实验和VIX波动率指数实证验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维时间序列回归中（p >> n），现有方法要么需要估计高维协方差矩阵（计算成本高且不稳定），要么面临非均匀渐近性和多重比较校正问题。本文旨在构造一个无需协方差矩阵估计、无需内生选择预测变量索引、也无需Bonferroni校正的检验方法，同时能处理弱依赖、异质数据。

Method: 构造基于最大值统计量的块自助检验（block bootstrap max-test）。假设数据满足物理依赖（physical dependence）条件，允许p以指数速率增长（ln(p) = o(n^a)）。采用乘子（wild）块自助法近似最大统计量的非标准极限分布，完全绕过协方差矩阵估计和自适应重采样步骤。

Result: 数值实验表明该max-test对尺寸控制良好，对各类备择假设（包括弱信号、稀疏信号）具有良好检验功效。VIX波动率指数的实证分析验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 该块自助最大检验为高维时间序列数据中的显著性预测变量检测提供了稳健且计算可行的方案，无需繁琐的协方差矩阵估计和多重比较校正，且能处理非平稳弱依赖数据。

Abstract: We construct a block bootstrap max-test for detecting the presence of significant predictors in a high dimensional setting, allowing for weakly dependent and heterogeneous (possibly non-stationary) data. The number of covariates to be screened may be large $p$ $>>$ $n$, and growing at an exponential rate, provided $\ln (p)$ $=$ $o(n^{a})$ for some $a$ $>$ $0$ that depends on memory decay and the growth of higher moments. We study the problem of correlation screening in a high dimensional marginal regression setting, assuming so-called \textit{physical dependence} in a time series setting. We entirely sidestep covariance matrix estimation and adaptive re-sampling by working with a max-statistic over the many computed parameters. Thus we do not need endogenous selection of the most relevant predictor index yielding non-uniform asymptotics, nor do we need a post-estimation Bonferroni correction. The non-standard limit distribution arising from the maximum of an increasing number of estimators is easily approximated by a multiplier (wild) block bootstrap. The max-test controls for size well, performs well against various deviations from the null, including very slight deviations with a weak or sparse signal. A numerical experiment is performed and an empirical example with the VIX volatility index is provided.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Learning Rate Transfer in Normalized Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27077)
*Boris Shigida,Boris Hanin,Andrey Gromov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出νGPT参数化方案，通过在nGPT基础上结合对齐指数（alignment exponents）修正μP方法，实现了学习率在模型宽度、深度和token范围上的跨规模迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: nGPT虽然训练速度快且无需权重衰减和warmup，但其学习率无法在模型维度和token范围内迁移，限制了超参数调优效率。

Method: 结合数值实验与对齐指数，重新审视并修正μP方法的超参数迁移策略，提出新的nGPT参数化方案νGPT。

Result: νGPT在宽度、深度和token范围上均实现了学习率迁移。

Conclusion: νGPT通过对齐指数修正μP方法，成功解决了nGPT学习率跨规模不可迁移的问题。

Abstract: The Normalized Transformer, or nGPT (arXiv:2410.01131) achieves impressive training speedups and does not require weight decay or learning rate warmup. However, despite having hyperparameters that explicitly scale with model size, we observe that nGPT does not exhibit learning rate transfer across model dimension and token horizon. To rectify this, we combine numerical experiments with a principled use of alignment exponents (arXiv:2407.05872) to revisit and modify the $μ$P approach to hyperparameter transfer (arXiv:2011.14522). The result is a novel nGPT parameterization we call $ν$GPT. Through extensive empirical validation, we find $ν$GPT exhibits learning rate transfer across width, depth, and token horizon.

</details>


### [167] [Optimized Deferral for Imbalanced Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27723)
*Corinna Cortes,Anqi Mao,Mehryar Mohri,Yutao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对两阶段学习延迟（learning to defer）中存在的专家不平衡问题，提出了一种基于边际的代价敏感学习方法MILD，通过将延迟损失优化转化为输入-专家域上的代价敏感学习问题，推导了新的边际损失函数和理论保证，在图像分类和LLM路由任务上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段学习延迟设置中，专家不平衡问题导致延迟算法偏向多数专家，造成次优性能，亟需一种能有效应对这一挑战的延迟算法。

Method: 将延迟损失优化重新定义为输入-专家域上的代价敏感学习问题，推导了面向该设定的新边际损失函数和理论保证，并基于此设计了MILD（Margin-based Imbalanced Learning to Defer）算法。

Result: 在图像分类和真实世界大语言模型路由任务上，MILD方法相较于现有基线取得了明显改进。

Conclusion: 本文提出的边际代价敏感学习方法有效解决了专家不平衡问题，为两阶段学习延迟提供了一种原则性且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning algorithms can be significantly improved by routing complex or uncertain inputs to specialized experts, balancing accuracy with computational cost. This approach, known as learning to defer, is essential in domains like natural language generation, medical diagnosis, and computer vision, where an effective deferral can reduce errors at low extra resource consumption. However, the two-stage learning to defer setting, which leverages existing predictors such as a collection of LLMs or other classifiers, often faces challenges due to an expert imbalance problem. This imbalance can lead to suboptimal performance, with deferral algorithms favoring the majority expert. We present a comprehensive study of two-stage learning to defer in expert imbalance settings. We cast the deferral loss optimization as a novel cost-sensitive learning problem over the input-expert domain. We derive new margin-based loss functions and guarantees tailored to this setting, and develop novel algorithms for cost-sensitive learning. Leveraging these results, we design principled deferral algorithms, MILD (Margin-based Imbalanced Learning to Defer), specifically suited for expert imbalance settings. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, showing clear improvements over existing baselines on both image classification and real-world Large Language Model (LLM) routing tasks.

</details>


### [168] [Linear-Core Surrogates: Smooth Loss Functions with Linear Rates for Classification and Structured Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27742)
*Mehryar Mohri,Yutao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Linear-Core (LC) Surrogates损失函数族，通过在线性核心外拼接平滑尾部，同时实现可微性和线性H-一致性界，在结构化预测中利用平滑性实现无偏随机梯度估计（避免O(|Y|²)复杂度），在序列标注任务上取得23倍加速，在带噪CIFAR-10上比交叉熵高2.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类损失函数存在根本性权衡：平滑损失（如交叉熵）优化速率快但一致性界为平方根速率，分段线性损失（如Hinge）一致性速率为线性但不可微。作者希望打破这一权衡，设计同时具备可微性和线性一致性速率的损失函数。

Method: 提出Linear-Core (LC) Surrogates：将线性核心与平滑尾部拼接，构造出处处可微且保持严格线性H-一致性界的凸损失函数。在结构化预测中，利用平滑性设计了无偏随机梯度估计器，避免精确推理的二次复杂度O(|Y|²)。

Result: 在大词汇量序列标注任务上，比Structured SVMs实现23倍加速；在带实例相关标签噪声的CIFAR-10上，比交叉熵高出2.6%的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LC Surrogates成功融合了平滑损失（优化优势）和基于间隔损失（统计效率）的优点，在计算效率和统计鲁棒性上均表现出显著优势。

Abstract: The choice of loss function in classification involves a fundamental trade-off: smooth losses (like Cross-Entropy) enable fast optimization rates but yield slow square-root consistency bounds, while piecewise-linear losses (like Hinge) offer fast linear consistency rates but suffer from non-differentiability. We propose Linear-Core (LC) Surrogates, a new family of convex loss functions that resolve this tension by stitching a linear core to a smooth tail. We prove that these surrogates are differentiable everywhere while retaining strict linear $H$-consistency bounds, effectively combining the optimization benefits of smoothness with the statistical efficiency of margin-based losses. In the structured prediction setting, we show that this smoothness unlocks a massive computational and energy advantage: it allows for an unbiased stochastic gradient estimator that bypasses the quadratic complexity $O(|\mathscr{Y}|^2)$ of exact inference (e.g., Viterbi). Empirically, our method achieves a 23$\times$ speedup over Structured SVMs on large-vocabulary sequence tagging tasks and demonstrates superior robustness to instance-dependent label noise, outperforming Cross-Entropy by 2.6% on corrupted CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [169] [Fidelity, Diversity, and Privacy: A Multi-Dimensional LLM Evaluation for Clinical Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27014)
*Guillermo Iglesias,Gema Bello-Orgaz,María Navas-Loro,Cristian Ramirez-Atencia,Mercè Salvador Robert,Enrique Baca-Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对心理健康领域标注数据稀缺及隐私限制问题，提出利用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5等LLM生成基于ICD-10编码的合成心理健康评估报告，并从语义保真度、词汇多样性、隐私/抄袭三个维度评估，结果表明模型能生成临床连贯、多样且隐私安全的合成数据，可扩展临床NLP训练数据而不损害患者机密性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量标注医疗数据（尤其是心理健康领域）稀缺，隐私法规限制数据共享，亟需一种不违反隐私规定且能生成高质量训练数据的替代方案。

Method: 利用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5三种LLM，在数据增强流水线中基于特定ICD-10编码生成合成心理健康评估报告；并构建综合评估框架，从语义保真度、词汇多样性、隐私/抄袭三个维度对生成的诊断文本进行评估。

Result: 所有模型均能生成临床连贯、词汇多样且隐私安全的合成报告，有效扩展了临床NLP任务的可用的训练数据量。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的合成数据生成是缓解心理健康领域数据稀缺问题的可行方案，可在保护患者隐私的前提下显著扩充高质量训练数据。

Abstract: The scarcity of high-quality annotated medical data, particularly in mental health, poses a significant bottleneck for training robust machine learning models. Privacy regulations restrict data sharing, making synthetic data generation a promising alternative. The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in a data augmentation pipeline could be leveraged as an alternative in this field. In the proposed methodology, DeepSeek-R1, OpenBioLLM-Llama3 and Qwen 3.5 are used to generate synthetic mental health evaluation reports conditioned on specific International Classification of Diseases, Tenth Revision (ICD-10) codes. Because naive text generation can lead to mode collapse or privacy breaches (memorization), a comprehensive evaluation framework is introduced. The generated diagnostic texts are assessed across three dimensions: semantic fidelity, lexical diversity, and privacy/plagiarism. The results demonstrate that all models can generate clinically coherent, diverse, and privacy-safe synthetic reports, significantly expanding the available training data for clinical natural language processing tasks without compromising patient confidentiality.

</details>


### [170] [Dynamic Adversarial Fine-Tuning Reorganizes Refusal Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27019)
*Wenhao Lan,Shan Li,Junbin Yang,Haihua Shen,Yijun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过测量驱动的方法，在7B骨干模型上研究了监督微调（SFT）和动态对抗微调（R2D2）对安全对齐语言模型中拒绝行为的影响。R2D2在早期完全抑制有害请求（ASR=0.000），但后期部分恢复（ASR=0.250），同时拒绝载体会从后期层迁移到前期层，有效秩保持在1.23-1.27的低维状态。结果表明拒绝行为存在重组织机制而非简单漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然刻画了拒绝方向和越狱鲁棒性，但未解释动态对抗微调如何随时间改变拒绝载体的变化机制，特别是训练过程中拒绝行为的动态演化规律尚不明确。

Method: 在7B骨干模型上，对SFT和R2D2两种方法进行对照实验；使用HarmBench、StrongREJECT和XSTest三种评估基准；构建五锚点拒绝几何套件进行几何分析；采用因果干预方法验证拒绝载体的因果作用。

Result: R2D2在步骤50和100时固定源HarmBench ASR降至0.000，步骤250升至0.035，步骤500升至0.250；SFT的ASR保持在0.505-0.588。XSTest中R2D2的任意拒绝率从1.000降至0.664和0.228。几何分析显示拒绝载体会从后期层迁移到前期层，有效秩约1.23-1.27。

Conclusion: 研究支持拒绝行为的重组织机制（reorganization account）而非简单漂移（drift-only account）的解释，但证据仅限于一个骨干模型和固定源攻击。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models must refuse harmful requests without collapsing into broad over-refusal, but the training-time mechanisms behind this tradeoff remain unclear. Prior work characterizes refusal directions and jailbreak robustness, yet does not explain how dynamic adversarial fine-tuning changes refusal carriers across training. We present a measurement-driven mechanism study, not a new defense, on one 7B backbone under supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and R2D2-style dynamic adversarial fine-tuning. Our protocol aligns fixed-source HarmBench, StrongREJECT, and XSTest with a five-anchor refusal-geometry suite and causal interventions. R2D2 drives fixed-source HarmBench ASR to 0.000 at steps 50 and 100, then partially reopens to 0.035 at step 250 and 0.250 at step 500; SFT remains less robust, with ASR between 0.505 and 0.588 at the same anchors. On XSTest, R2D2 any-refusal is 1.000 early, then falls to 0.664 and 0.228. Geometrically, R2D2 preserves a late-layer admissible carrier through step 100 before relocating to an early-layer carrier, while effective rank remains near 1.23--1.27. Causal interventions indicate low-dimensional but utility-coupled control. These results support a reorganization account rather than a drift-only account, with evidence limited to one backbone and fixed-source attacks.

</details>


### [171] [AdaBFL: Multi-Layer Defensive Adaptive Aggregation for Bzantine-Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27434)
*Zehui Tang,Yuchen Liu,Feihu Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AdaBFL的多层防御自适应聚合方法，用于拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习，通过三层防御机制自适应调整防御算法权重以对抗复杂攻击，并在非凸非独立同分布数据设置下证明了收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习易受投毒攻击，现有拜占庭鲁棒方法难以在多种攻击类型间提供平衡防御，或依赖于服务器拥有数据集，因此需要一种不依赖服务器数据集且能自适应应对多种攻击的鲁棒聚合方法。

Method: 提出AdaBFL方法，采用新颖的三层防御机制，能够自适应调整各防御算法的权重以应对复杂攻击；并在非凸设置下对非独立同分布数据提供了收敛性证明。

Result: 在多个数据集上的综合实验表明，AdaBFL方法优于对比算法。

Conclusion: AdaBFL通过多层自适应防御机制有效解决了联邦学习中对多种投毒攻击的平衡防御问题，且不需要服务器持有数据集，具备理论收敛保证和实证优越性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is a popular distributed learning paradigm in machine learning, which enables multiple clients to collaboratively train models under the guidance of a server without exposing private client data. However, FL's decentralized nature makes it vulnerable to poisoning attacks, where malicious clients can submit corrupted models to manipulate the system. To counter such attacks, although various Byzantine-robust methods have been proposed, these methods struggle to provide balanced defense against multiple types of attacks or rely on possessing the dataset in the server. To deal with these drawbacks, thus, we propose an effective multi-layer defensive adaptive aggregation for Bzantine-robust federated learning (AdaBFL) based on a novel three-layer defensive mechanism, which can adaptively adjust the weights of defense algorithms to counter complex attacks. Moreover, we provide convergence properties of our AdaBFL method under the non-convex setting on non-iid data. Comprehensive experiments across multiple datasets validate the superiority of our AdaBFL over the comparable algorithms.

</details>


### [172] [Low Rank Adaptation for Adversarial Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27487)
*Han Liu,Shanghao Shi,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,Chongjie Zhang,Ning Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和大量实验证明，对抗性扰动具有与LoRA类似的低秩结构，并利用这一特性将黑盒对抗攻击的搜索限制在低秩子空间中，显著提升了攻击效率和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受LoRA在LLM训练中利用低秩矩阵提升效率的启发，探究对抗性扰动是否同样具有低秩结构，并利用该特性改善黑盒对抗攻击中查询效率低下的问题。

Method: 两步法：首先利用参考模型和辅助数据将梯度投影到低维子空间；然后将黑盒攻击中的扰动搜索限制在该低秩子空间内，从而提升攻击效率和效果。

Result: 在多种攻击方法、模型架构、数据集和威胁模型下，所提出的低秩对抗攻击方法相比传统方法在性能上取得了一致且显著的提升。

Conclusion: 对抗性扰动具有内在的低秩结构，利用该结构可以有效改进黑盒对抗攻击的查询效率与攻击效果。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), which leverages the insight that model updates typically reside in a low-dimensional space, has significantly improved the training efficiency of Large Language Models (LLMs) by updating neural network layers using low-rank matrices. Since the generation of adversarial examples is an optimization process analogous to model training, this naturally raises the question: Do adversarial perturbations exhibit a similar low-rank structure?
  In this paper, we provide both theoretical analysis and extensive empirical investigation across various attack methods, model architectures, and datasets to show that adversarial perturbations indeed possess an inherently low-rank structure. This insight opens up new opportunities for improving both adversarial attacks and defenses. We mainly focus on leveraging this low-rank property to improve the efficiency and effectiveness of black-box adversarial attacks, which often suffer from excessive query requirements. Our method follows a two-step approach. First, we use a reference model and auxiliary data to guide the projection of gradients into a low-dimensional subspace. Next, we confine the perturbation search in black-box attacks to this low-rank subspace, significantly improving the efficiency and effectiveness of the adversarial attacks.
  We evaluated our approach across a range of attack methods, benchmark models, datasets, and threat models. The results demonstrate substantial and consistent improvements in the performance of our low-rank adversarial attacks compared to conventional methods.

</details>


### [173] [Monitoring Neural Training with Topology: A Footprint-Predictable Collapse Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26984)
*Alexander Kalinowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拓扑感知的在线监测方法（MMHM + CI），用于检测神经表示中的表征坍塌问题，该方法通过稀疏编辑和离散Morse匹配实现快速增量更新，在LLM微调和时序KGE训练中可作为低延迟的早期预警信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征坍塌（Representational collapse）会导致嵌入变得各向异性并失去多尺度结构，且这一问题在性能指标尚未反应时就已经损害下游性能，因此需要一种能够在训练过程中实时监测的早期预警方法。

Method: 提出了模块化Morse同调维护（MMHM）与复合坍塌指数（CI）相结合的方法：不重建整个复形，而是在固定尺度下进行稀疏编辑并维持离散Morse匹配，从而实现快速的增量更新。

Result: 在LLM微调和时序知识图谱嵌入（KGE）训练中，CI提供了低延迟的早期预警信号，适合在训练过程中进行干预。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的在线监测方法（MMHM + CI）能有效检测表征坍塌，且计算开销低，适合实际训练场景中的实时干预。

Abstract: Representational collapse, where embeddings become anisotropic and lose multi-scale structure, can erode downstream performance long before performance metrics react. We propose an online, topology-aware monitor for evolving neural representations that couples Modular Morse Homology Maintenance (MMHM) with a composite Collapse Index (CI). Instead of rebuilding complexes each epoch, we apply sparse edits at a fixed scale and maintain a discrete Morse matching, yielding fast, incremental updates. Across LLM fine-tuning and temporal KGE training, CI provides a low-latency early-warning signal suitable for in-training interventions. Code and experimental scripts will be released publicly

</details>


### [174] [Simple Self-Conditioning Adaptation for Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26985)
*Michael Cardei,Huu Binh Ta,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自条件掩码扩散模型（SCMDM），通过在去噪步骤中引入模型自身之前的干净状态预测作为条件，无需重新训练即可显著提升掩码扩散模型的生成质量，在多个领域（文本、图像、分子、基因组）均取得一致改进，生成困惑度降低近50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码扩散模型中，若某位置在反向更新后仍处于掩码状态，模型会丢弃该位置之前的干净状态预测，迫使这些位置仅从掩码token反复推断，限制了跨步骤的细化能力。

Method: 提出SCMDM，一种后训练适应方法：在每次去噪步骤中，将模型上一轮对干净序列的预测作为当前步骤的额外条件输入，仅需最小化架构改动（引入一个可训练的线性投影层将预测嵌入映射到模型维度并加和到掩码token表示上），无需循环隐状态路径、无需辅助参考模型、不增加采样时的去噪器评估次数。

Result: 在OWT文本模型上生成困惑度从42.89降至23.72（降幅近50%）；在离散图像生成、小分子生成、基因组分布建模等多个领域均显著优于标准MDM基线。

Conclusion: 部分自条件方法（含50% dropout策略）在后训练场景下是次优的；一旦模型的自生成干净状态估计变得信息丰富，专精于细化目标（而非混合条件/无条件目标）更为优越。SCMDM提供了一种轻量级、无需从头训练的有效改进范式。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate discrete sequences by iterative denoising under an absorbing masking process. In standard masked diffusion, if a token remains masked after a reverse update, the model discards its clean-state prediction for that position. Thus, still-masked positions must be repeatedly inferred from the mask token alone. This design choice limits cross-step refinement. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a simple, yet effective, post-training adaptation for MDMs that conditions each denoising step on the model's own previous clean-state predictions. The resulting method, called Self-Conditioned Masked Diffusion Models (SCMDM), requires minimal architectural change, does not introduce a recurrent latent-state pathway, does not rely on an auxiliary reference model, and adds no extra denoiser evaluations during sampling. This is an important departure from partial self-conditioning approaches which requires expensive model training from scratch. In particular, the paper shows that partial self-conditioning, including the commonly used 50% dropout strategy for training self-conditioned models from scratch, is suboptimal in the post-training regime. Instead, once the model's self-generated clean-state estimates become informative, the specialization to refinement is preferable to mixing conditional and unconditional objectives. SCMDM is evaluated across multiple domains, demonstrating consistent improvement over vanilla MDM baselines, achieving nearly a 50% reduction in generative perplexity on OWT-trained models (42.89 to 23.72), alongside strong improvements in discretized image synthesis quality, small molecular generation, and enhanced fidelity in genomic distribution modeling.

</details>


### [175] [People-Centred Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26991)
*Zheng Zhang,Milad Masroor,Cuong Nguyen,Tahir Hassan,Yuanhong Chen,David Rosewarne,Kevin Wells,Thanh-Toan Do,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan)框架，通过动态门控机制联合优化公平性、诊断准确性和工作流效率，并引入FairHAI基准进行评估，实验表明PecMan优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心的医疗AI虽取得高准确性，但缺乏临床采纳，原因在于对跨人群公平性能和工作流整合的优化不足；现有工作流整合（L2D、L2C）与AI公平性研究相互孤立，忽视了它们的相互依赖性和临床实践约束（如临床医生可用性受限）。

Method: 提出PecMan框架，采用动态门控机制，在临床医生工作负载约束下将病例分配给AI、临床医生或两者共同处理，联合优化公平性、诊断准确性和工作流效果。

Result: 引入FairHAI基准评估准确性、公平性和临床医生工作负载之间的权衡，实验表明PecMan持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PecMan为更可信和临床可行的AI系统铺平了道路，强调了公平性与工作流整合联合优化的必要性。

Abstract: Recent advances in data-centric medical AI have produced highly accurate diagnostic systems, but the emphasis on data curation and performance metrics has not translated into widespread clinical adoption. We conjecture that this limited uptake stems from insufficient attention dedicated to the optimisation of fair performance across diverse patient populations and to workflow integration: performance biases can create regulatory barriers, and poorly integrated automation can disrupt clinical routines, degrade the quality of human-AI collaboration, and reduce clinicians' willingness to adopt AI tools. Prior work on workflow integration (e.g., Learning to Defer (L2D) and Learning to Complement (L2C)) and AI fairness has typically examined these challenges in isolation, overlooking their natural interdependence and the practical constraints of clinical environments, such as restricted clinician availability. We propose People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan), a human-AI framework that jointly optimises fairness, diagnostic accuracy, and workflow effectiveness through a dynamic gating mechanism that assigns cases to AI, clinicians, or both under clinician workload constraints. We also introduce the Fairness and Human-Centred AI (FairHAI) benchmark for evaluating trade-offs between accuracy, fairness, and clinician workload. Experiments using this benchmark show that PecMan consistently outperforms existing methods, paving the way for more trustworthy and clinically viable AI systems. Code will be available upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [176] [Automatic Causal Fairness Analysis with LLM-Generated Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27011)
*Alessia Berarducci,Eric Rossetto,Alessandro Antonucci,Marco Zaffalon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairMind原型工具，基于反事实因果公平模型自动分析数据集层面的公平性，利用LLM生成零样本公平性报告，并支持有序保护变量和连续目标的新分解结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数AutoML框架未考虑训练数据和预测中潜在的公平性缺失问题，需要一种能够自动进行数据集层面公平性分析的方法。

Method: 基于Plečko和Bareinboim提出的标准公平模型（反事实因果效应），通过封闭形式计算受保护特征对目标的因果效应，利用LLM在零样本设置下生成公平性分析报告。

Result: 通过示例展示了相较于LLM直接分析的优势，并扩展了有序保护变量和连续目标的新分解结果。

Conclusion: FairMind能够实现数据集层面的自动化公平性评估，为AutoML框架融入公平性分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AutoML, intended as the process of automating the application of machine learning to real-world problems, is a key step for AI popularisation. Most AutoML frameworks are not accounting for the potential lack of fairness in the training data and in the corresponding predictions. We introduce \textsc{FairMind}, a software prototype aiming to automatise fairness analysis at the dataset level. We achieve that by resorting to the assumptions of the \emph{standard fairness model}, recently proposed by Plečko and Bareinboim. This allows for a sound fairness evaluation in terms of causal effects, based on \emph{counterfactual} queries involving the target, possibly confounders and mediators, and the different values of an input feature we regard as \emph{protected}. After the necessary data preprocessing, the tool implements a closed-form computation of the effects. LLMs are consequently exploited to generate accurate reports on the fairness levels detected in the training dataset. We achieve that in a zero-shot setup and show by examples the expected advantages with respect to a direct analysis performed by the LLM. To favour applications, extensions to ordinal protected variable and continuous targets and novel decomposition results are also discussed.

</details>


### [177] [Cross-Subject Generalization for EEG Decoding: A Survey of Deep Learning Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27033)
*Taida Li,Yujun Yan,Fei Dou,Wenzhan Song,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了针对跨被试脑电解码中个体差异导致的域偏移问题而设计的深度学习方法，将其归类为特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦和对比学习四大方法论家族，并讨论了当前方法的理论局限、被试身份的结构性价值以及脑电基础模型的兴起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试脑电解码面临严重的个体间域偏移问题，制约了深度学习模型向未见被试的泛化能力，亟需系统性梳理和评估现有解决方法。

Method: 将跨被试问题形式化为多源域问题，建立严格的被试独立评估协议，并对现有文献按特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦和对比学习四大方法论家族进行系统分类与综述。

Result: 给出了跨被试脑电解码深度学习方法的系统性分类体系，明确了各方法家族的定位及其在域泛化问题中的适用性。

Conclusion: 当前方法存在理论局限性，被试身份信息具有结构性价值，脑电基础模型是推进鲁棒真实世界解码的关键方向。

Abstract: Deep learning for cross-subject EEG decoding is hindered by high inter-subject variability, which introduces a severe domain shift between training and unseen test subjects. This survey presents a comprehensive review of deep learning methodologies specifically engineered to address this cross-subject generalization challenge. To ground this analysis, we formalize the cross-subject setting as a multi-source domain problem and delineate the rigorous, subject-independent evaluation protocols required for valid assessment. Central to this survey is a systematic taxonomy of the current literature into discrete methodological families, including feature alignment, adversarial learning, feature disentanglement, and contrastive learning. We conclude by examining three critical elements for advancing robust, real-world decoding: the theoretical limitations of current methodologies, the structural value of subject identity, and the emergence of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [178] [Detecting Clinical Discrepancies in Health Coaching Agents: A Dual-Stream Memory and Reconciliation Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27045)
*Samuel L Pugh,Eric Yang,Alexander Muir Sutherland,Alessandra Breschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双流记忆架构，将患者自述与结构化临床记录（FHIR）严格分离，并通过调和引擎检测两者间的差异。在26名患者、675次纵向健康辅导会话中，该引擎对临床差异的检测率为84.4%，安全关键召回率为86.7%，同时揭示了13.6%的错误级联现象——主要源于非结构化对话中记忆提取环节的临床细节丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的通用记忆系统倾向于用患者最新陈述覆盖旧信息以保持连贯性，这在临床场景中可能导致安全隐患。患者的自我报告虽然实时但存在回忆偏差，而电子健康记录（EHR）虽经医学验证却常常过时，两种信息源的不一致需要专门的记忆架构来调和。

Method: 提出双流记忆架构（Dual-Stream Memory Architecture）：将患者叙事与结构化临床记录（FHIR格式）严格分离存储；设计专门的调和引擎（Reconciliation Engine），对每条提取的记忆与患者FHIR档案进行对比，按类型、严重程度及涉及的FHIR资源对差异进行分类。实验使用26名患者、675次纵向健康指导会话的混合数据集，包含真实医患对话与基于FHIR的合成临床场景。

Result: 调和引擎在隔离测试中检测到84.4%的设计性临床差异，安全关键召回率达86.7%。通过将提取和调和评估在同一数据上耦合，直接量化出13.6%的错误级联，溯源表明性能退化主要源于从非结构化对话中提取记忆时的临床细节丢失，而非下游分类错误。

Conclusion: 验证了将患者报告的记忆与临床记录进行比对在技术上是可行的，对于纵向健康智能体的安全部署是必要的。错误级联的分析揭示了改进方向应聚焦于记忆提取环节对临床细节的保真度。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents transition from single-session tools to persistent systems managing longitudinal healthcare journeys, their memory architectures face a critical challenge: reconciling two imperfect sources of truth. The patient's evolving self-report is current but prone to recall bias, while the Electronic Health Record (EHR) is medically validated but frequently stale. General-purpose agent memory systems optimize for coherence by overwriting older facts with the user's latest statement, a pattern that risks safety failures when applied to clinical data. We introduce a Dual-Stream Memory Architecture that strictly separates the patient narrative from the structured clinical record (FHIR), governed by a dedicated Reconciliation Engine that evaluates every extracted memory against the patient's FHIR profile and classifies discrepancies by type, severity, and the specific FHIR resources involved. We evaluate this architecture on 26 patients across 675 longitudinal wellness coaching sessions, using a hybrid dataset that interleaves real provider-patient transcripts with synthetic, FHIR-grounded clinical scenarios. In isolated testing, the engine detects 84.4% of designed clinical discrepancies with 86.7% safety-critical recall. By coupling extraction and reconciliation evaluation on the same data, we directly quantify a 13.6% error cascade, tracing the degradation to clinical details lost during memory extraction from unstructured conversation rather than to downstream classification errors. These findings establish that validating patient-reported memories against clinical records is both feasible and necessary for safe deployment of longitudinal health agents.

</details>


### [179] [Co-Evolving Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27083)
*Naibin Gu,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Chuanyu Qin,Dingyu Yao,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoPD方法，通过并行训练专家并引入在线策略蒸馏，实现文本、图像、视频推理能力的全合一整合，优于混合RLVR和MOPD等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR和OPD两种范式在整合多专家能力时存在不同缺陷：混合RLVR存在跨能力发散代价，而先训练专家再OPD的流水线方式因师生行为模式差距大而无法充分吸收教师能力。因此需要一种能同时避免这两种缺陷的统一方法。

Method: 提出协同进化策略蒸馏（CoPD），在每位专家进行RLVR训练的过程中并行引入OPD（而非在专家完全训练后），专家之间互为教师（使OPD双向化），实现协同进化，从而在保持充分互补知识的同时获得更一致的行为模式。

Result: CoPD实现了文本、图像、视频推理能力的全合一整合，显著优于混合RLVR和MOPD等强基线，甚至超越了领域特定专家模型。

Conclusion: CoPD的模型并行训练模式可能启发一种新的训练缩放范式，为多能力融合的后训练提供了更有效的统一方案。

Abstract: RLVR and OPD have become standard paradigms for post-training. We provide a unified analysis of these two paradigms in consolidating multiple expert capabilities into a single model, identifying capability loss in different ways: mixed RLVR suffers from inter-capability divergence cost, while the pipeline of first training experts and then performing OPD, though avoiding divergence, fails to fully absorb teacher capabilities due to large behavioral pattern gaps between teacher and student. We propose Co-Evolving Policy Distillation (CoPD), which encourages parallel training of experts and introduces OPD during each expert's ongoing RLVR training rather than after complete expert training, with experts serving as mutual teachers (making OPD bidirectional) to co-evolve. This enables more consistent behavioral patterns among experts while maintaining sufficient complementary knowledge throughout. Experiments validate that CoPD achieves all-in-one integration of text, image, and video reasoning capabilities, significantly outperforming strong baselines such as mixed RLVR and MOPD, and even surpassing domain-specific experts. The model parallel training pattern offered by CoPD may inspire a novel training scaling paradigm.

</details>


### [180] [AutoSP: Unlocking Long-Context LLM Training Via Compiler-Based Sequence Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27089)
*Ahan Gupta,Zhihao Wang,Neel Dani,Masahiro Tanaka,Olatunji Ruwase,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoSP是首个自动优化LLM长上下文训练的解决方案，通过自动序列并行和长上下文感知的激活检查点技术，在NVIDIA和AMD硬件上分别将训练上下文长度提升至2.7倍和2.5倍，且运行时性能损失可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练库（如ZeRO-3/FSDP、张量并行和流水线并行）主要针对大参数模型优化，缺乏对长上下文训练的高层抽象支持。用户需要手动组合序列并行等复杂优化技术，这需要深厚的专业知识，降低了开发效率。

Method: AutoSP采用自动化编译方法，实现两大核心优化：1）自动序列并行（Automated Sequence Parallelism），自动将序列维度切分到多个设备；2）长上下文感知的激活检查点（Long-Context Aware Activation-Checkpointing），针对长序列特性优化内存使用。

Result: 在NVIDIA硬件上训练上下文长度提升至2.7倍，在AMD硬件上提升至2.5倍，且运行时性能开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: AutoSP通过自动化组合序列并行和激活检查点优化，显著增强了LLM在长上下文场景下的可训练性，同时降低了用户实现复杂优化的门槛，在多种硬件平台上均取得了显著效果。

Abstract: Large-language-models (LLMs) demonstrate enormous utility in long-context tasks which require processing prompts that consist of tens to hundreds of thousands of tokens. However, existing LLM training libraries do not provide easy to use abstractions to optimize for long-context training, instead focusing on optimizations for models with large parameter counts through ZeRO-3/FSDP, Tensor and Pipeline parallelism. This forces users to rewrite LLM training libraries to incorporate compositions of various complex long-context optimizations, such as sequence-parallelism, to training pipelines; a process that requires in-depth expertise, reducing developer productivity. To tackle these challenges, we introduce AutoSP: the first automated solution to automatically optimize LLM training for longer-contexts. AutoSP compiles models and applies a targeted set of optimizations: automated sequence parallelism, and long-context aware activation-checkpointing, to drastically enhance LLM trainability at negligible cost to throughput. Our evaluation demonstrates AutoSP's capability on both NVIDIA and AMD hardware, increasing training contexts by upto 2.7$\times$ and 2.5$\times$ respectively over competitive hand-written baseline at negligible cost to runtime performance.

</details>


### [181] [Anomaly Detection in Soil Heavy Metal Contamination Using Unsupervised Learning for Environmental Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27102)
*Isaac Tettey Adjokatse,Samuel Senyo Koranteng,George Yamoah Afrifa,Theophilus Ansah-Narh,Marcellin Atemkeng,Joseph Bremang Tandoh,Kow Ahor Essel-Yorke,Richmond Opoku-Sarkodie,Rebecca Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究采用无监督机器学习框架（Isolation Forest、PCA重构误差、DBSCAN及共识方法）检测加纳中部地区12个垃圾场土壤中八种重金属的异常污染模式。共识方法识别出6个稳健异常样本（7.7%），全部集中于S3站点，其平均HI值比正常样本高70–80%，且均超过HI=1阈值。PCA重构误差与HI强正相关（r≈0.8）。识别出三种异常类型：S3的极端Cu富集、S4/S5的异常低Ni、S9–S12的多金属（Pb–Zn）中度共升高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
